   p.add_argument(\\\"--max_steps\\\", type=int, default=300,\\n                   help=\\\"Max steps per rollout\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n \\\"crm_perdof\\\", \\\"reconvla\\\"])\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--smolvla_path\\\", type=str, default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero/\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--obs_dim\\\", type=int, default=OBS_DIM)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--hidden_dim\\\", type=int, default=256)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--render\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output_file\\\", type=str, default=None,\\n                   help=\\\"Save results JSON to this path\\\")\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef get_robot_state(obs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract 8D robot state: eef_pos(3) + eef_axisangle(3) + gripper_qpos(2).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    eef_pos = obs[\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\"]\\n    eef_axisangle = quat2axisangle(obs[\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\"])\\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\"]\\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float64)\\n\\n\\ndef load_libero_env(benchmark_name):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load LIBERO benchmark tasks and create environments.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from libero.libero import benchmark as bm\\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\n\\n    benchmark_dict = {\\n        \\\"spatial\\\": \\\"libero_spatial\\\",\\n        \\\"object\\\": \\\"libero_object\\\",\\n        \\\"goal\\\": \\\"libero_goal\\\",\\n        \\\"long\\\": \\\"libero_10\\\",\\n        \\\"90\\\": \\\"libero_90\\\",\\n    }\\n    bm_name = benchmark_dict[benchmark_name]\\n    benchmark_instance = bm.get_benchmark(bm_name)()\\n    n_tasks = benchmark_instance.n_tasks\\n\\n    task_envs = []\\n    for task_idx in range(n_tasks):\\n        task = benchmark_instance.get_task(task_idx)\\n        task_name = task.name\\n        task_description = task.language\\n        task_bddl = benchmark_instance.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_idx)\\n\\n        env_args = {\\n            \\\"bddl_file_name\\\": task_bddl,\\n            \\\"camera_heights\\\": 256,\\n            \\\"camera_widths\\\": 256,\\n            \\\"camera_names\\\": [\\\"agentview\\\", \\\"robot0_eye_in_hand\\\"],\\n            \\\"render_gpu_device_id\\\": 0,\\n        }\\n        env =  \\\"env\\\": env,\\n            \\\"init_states\\\": benchmark_instance.get_task_init_states(task_idx),\\n        })\\n\\n    return task_envs\\n\\n\\ndef select_action_vla_only(wrapper, obs, task_name, K, cgs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Baseline: mean of K samples (full 7D), no CRM.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    agentview = obs[\\\"agentview_image\\\"]\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\", None)\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\n\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\n        task_name=task_name,\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\n        K=K,\\n    )\\n    # samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM)\\n    k_trunc = samples[0, :, :H_EFF, :]  # (K, H_eff, 7)\\n    mean_action = k_trunc.mean(dim=0)  # (H_eff, 7)\\n    return mean_action[0].cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef select_action_crm(wrapper, obs, task_name, K, cgs, crm, device):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"ACR-VLA: CGS on 6D pose → CRM refinement → combine with mean gripper.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    agentview = obs[\\\"agentview_image\\\"]\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\", None)\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\n\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\n mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)  # (H_eff, 1)\\n    selected_full = torch.cat([selected_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\n    return selected_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef rollout_single(env, init_state, action_fn, max_steps=300):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run one rollout. action_fn returns 7D action directly.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\\n \\\"crm_perdof\\\", \\\"reconvla\\\"])\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--smolvla_path\\\", type=str, default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero/\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--obs_dim\\\", type=int, default=OBS_DIM)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--hidden_dim\\\", type=int, default=256)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--render\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output_file\\\", type=str, default=None,\\n                   help=\\\"Save results JSON to this path\\\")\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef get_robot_state(obs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract 8D robot state: eef_pos(3) + eef_axisangle(3) + gripper_qpos(2).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    eef_pos = obs[\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\"]\\n    eef_axisangle = quat2axisangle(obs[\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\"])\\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\"]\\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float64)\\n\\n\\ndef load_libero_env(benchmark_name):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load LIBERO benchmark tasks and create environments.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from libero.libero import benchmark as bm\\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\n\\n    benchmark_dict = {\\n        \\\"spatial\\\": \\\"libero_spatial\\\",\\n        \\\"object\\\": \\\"libero_object\\\",\\n        \\\"goal\\\": \\\"libero_goal\\\",\\n        \\\"long\\\": \\\"libero_10\\\",\\n        \\\"90\\\": \\\"libero_90\\\",\\n    }\\n    bm_name = benchmark_dict[benchmark_name]\\n    benchmark_instance = bm.get_benchmark(bm_name)()\\n    n_tasks = benchmark_instance.n_tasks\\n\\n    task_envs = []\\n    for task_idx in range(n_tasks):\\n        task = benchmark_instance.get_task(task_idx)\\n        task_name = task.name\\n        task_description = task.language\\n        task_bddl = benchmark_instance.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_idx)\\n\\n        env_args = {\\n            \\\"bddl_file_name\\\": task_bddl,\\n            \\\"camera_heights\\\": 256,\\n            \\\"camera_widths\\\": 256,\\n            \\\"camera_names\\\": [\\\"agentview\\\", \\\"robot0_eye_in_hand\\\"],\\n            \\\"render_gpu_device_id\\\": 0,\\n        }\\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\n        env.seed(42)\\n\\n        task_envs.append({\\n            \\\"task_name\\\": task_name,\\n            \\\"task_description\\\": task_description,\\n            \\\"env\\\": env,\\n            \\\"init_states\\\": benchmark_instance.get_task_init_states(task_idx),\\n        })\\n\\n    return task_envs\\n\\n\\ndef select_action_vla_only(wrapper, obs, task_name, K, cgs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Baseline: mean of K samples (full 7D), no CRM.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    agentview = obs[\\\"agentview_image\\\"]\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\", None)\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\n\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\n        task_name=task_name,\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\n        K=K,\\n    )\\n    # samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM)\\n    k_trunc = samples[0, :, :H_EFF, :]  # (K, H_eff, 7)\\n    mean_action = k_trunc.mean(dim=0)  # (H_eff, 7)\\n    return mean_action[0].cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef select_action_crm(wrapper, obs, task_name, K, cgs, crm, device):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"ACR-VLA: CGS on 6D pose → CRM refinement → combine with mean gripper.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    agentview = obs[\\\"agentview_image\\\"]\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\", None)\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\n\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\n refined_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef select_action_reconvla(wrapper, obs, task_name, K):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"ReconVLA baseline: scalar Euclidean argmin on 6D pose, pass-through gripper.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    agentview = obs[\\\"agentview_image\\\"]\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\", None)\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\n\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\n        task_name=task_name,\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\n mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)  # (H_eff, 1)\\n    selected_full = torch.cat([selected_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\n    return selected_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nConformalized Gaussian Scoring (CGS) module.\\n\\nInput: K flow-matching samples, shape (K, H_eff, D) where D=CORRECTION_DIM=6, H_eff=5.\\n       Accepts (K, chunk_size, D') and auto-truncates to (K, H_eff, action_dim).\\n\\nOutput:\\n  - uncertainty_features: (action_dim*H_eff + H_eff + H_eff) tensor\\n    [per_dof_std(30), conformal_radius(5), log_volume(5)]\\n  - mean_action: (H_eff*action_dim) tensor (K samples mean, flattened)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport torch\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom sklearn.covariance import LedoitWolf\\n\\ntry:\\n    from utils import CORRECTION_DIM\\nexcept ImportError:\\n    from .utils import CORRECTION_DIM\\n\\n\\nclass ConformilizedGaussianScoring:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute structured uncertainty features from K flow-matching samples (pose only).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, K: int = 10, horizon: int = 5, action_dim: int = CORRECTION_DIM,\\n                 alpha: float = 0.1, chunk_size: int = 50):\\n        self.K = K\\n        self.horizon = horizon       # H_eff = 5\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim  # 6D pose (no gripper)\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.chunk_size = chunk_size\\n\\n    def compute_features(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Args:\\n            samples: (K, T, D') where T >= H_eff and D' >= action_dim.\\n                     Auto-truncates to (K, H_eff, action_dim).\\n        Returns:\\n            uncertainty_features: (action_dim*H_eff + H_eff + H_eff,)\\n            mean_action: (H_eff * action_dim,) — flattened mean over K samples\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if samples.shape[1] > self.horizon:\\n            samples = samples[:, :self.horizon, :]\\n        if samples.shape[2] > self.action_dim:\\n            samples = samples[:, :, :self.action_dim]\\n\\n        assert samples.shape == (self.K, self.horizon, self.action_dim), \\\\\\n            f\\\"Expected ({self.K}, {self.horizon}, {self.action_dim}), got {samples.shape}\\\"\\n\\n        mean_action = samples.mean(dim=0)  # (H, D)\\n\\n        per_dof_stds = []\\n        conformal_radii = []\\n        log_volumes = []\\n\\n        for h in range(self.horizon):\\n            step_samples = samples[:, h, :]  # (K, D)\\n            cov_matrix = self._ledoit_wolf_cov(step_samples)  # (D, D)\\n\\n            stds = torch.sqrt(torch.diag(cov_matrix).clamp(min=1e-10))\\n            per_dof_stds.append(stds)\\n\\n            q_h = self._conformal_radius(step_samples, mean_action[h], cov_matrix)\\n            conformal_radii.append(q_h)\\n\\n            sign, logabsdet = torch.linalg.slogdet(cov_matrix)\\n            log_vol = 0.5 * logabsdet\\n            log_volumes.append(log_vol)\\n\\n        # 6*5=30 + 5 + 5 = 40D\\n        uncertainty_features = torch.cat([\\n            torch.cat(per_dof_stds),       # (30,)\\n            torch.stack(conformal_radii),   # (5,)\\n            torch.stack(log_volumes),       # (5,)\\n        ])\\n\\n        return uncertainty_features, mean_action.flatten()\\n\\n    def compute_features_batch(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Batch version: process B sets of K samples.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        B = samples.shape[0]\\n        all_uf = []\\n        all_ma = []\\n        for b in range(B):\\n            uf, ma = self.compute_features(samples[b])\\n            all_uf.append(uf)\\n            all_ma.append(ma)\\n        return torch.stack(all_uf), torch.stack(all_ma)\\n\\n    def _ledoit_wolf_cov(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        samples_np = samples.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n        lw = LedoitWolf().fit(samples_np)\\n        return torch.tensor(lw.covariance_, dtype=samples.dtype, device=samples.device)\\n\\n    def _conformal_radius(self, samples: torch.Tensor, mean: torch.Tensor,\\n                          cov: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        diff = samples - mean.unsqueeze(0)\\n        cov_inv = torch.linalg.inv(cov + 1e-6 * torch.eye(cov.shape[0], device=cov.device))\\n        mahal = torch.sqrt((diff @ cov_inv * diff).sum(dim=1).clamp(min=0))\\n\\n        q_idx = int(np.ceil((1 - self.alpha) * (self.K + 1))) - 1\\n        q_idx = min(q_idx, self.K - 1)\\n        q_h = torch.sort(mahal)[0][q_idx]\\n        return q_h\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}

(D, D)\\n\\n            stds = torch.sqrt(torch.diag(cov_matrix).clamp(min=1e-10))\\n            per_dof_stds.append(stds)\\n\\n            q_h = self._conformal_radius(step_samples, mean_action[h], cov_matrix)\\n            conformal_radii.append(q_h)\\n\\n            sign, logabsdet = torch.linalg.slogdet(cov_matrix)\\n            log_vol = 0.5 * logabsdet\\n            log_volumes.append(log_vol)\\n\\n        # 6*5=30 + 5 + 5 = 40D\\n        uncertainty_features = torch.cat([\\n            torch.cat(per_dof_stds),       # (30,)\\n            torch.stack(conformal_radii),   # (5,)\\n            torch.stack(log_volumes),       # (5,)\\n        ])\\n\\n        return uncertainty_features, mean_action.flatten()\\n\\n    def compute_features_batch(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Batch version: process B sets of K samples.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        B = samples.shape[0]\\n        all_uf = []\\n        all_ma = []\\n        for b in range(B):\\n            uf, ma = self.compute_features(samples[b])\\n            all_uf.append(uf)\\n            all_ma.append(ma)\\n        return torch.stack(all_uf), torch.stack(all_ma)\\n\\n    def _ledoit_wolf_cov(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        samples_np = samples.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n        lw = LedoitWolf().fit(samples_np)\\n        return torch.tensor(lw.covariance_, dtype=samples.dtype, device=samples.device)\\n\\n    def _conformal_radius(self, samples: torch.Tensor, mean: torch.Tensor,\\n                          cov: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        diff = samples - mean.unsqueeze(0)\\n        cov_inv = torch.linalg.inv(cov + 1e-6 * torch.eye(cov.shape[0], device=cov.device))\\n        mahal = torch.sqrt((diff @ cov_inv * diff).sum(dim=1).clamp(min=0))\\n\\n        q_idx = int(np.ceil((1 - self.alpha) * (self.K + 1))) - 1\\n        q_idx = min(q_idx, self.K - 1)\\n        q_h = torch.sort(mahal)[0][q_idx]\\n        return q_h\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"
    }
  }
}

{
  "server": "bjb1-18228",
  "command": "cat > /tmp/patch_evaluate.py << 'PYEOF'\nimport re\n\nwith open(\"/root/acr-vla-conformal-refinement/src/evaluate.py\", \"r\") as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\n# 1. Update docstring: add acr_vla mode description\nold_baselines = '''Baselines:\n  - \"vla_only\": SmolVLA mean of K=10, no CRM refinement\n  - \"crm_scalar\": CRM with scalar uncertainty (single std, not per-DOF)\n  - \"crm_perdof\": CRM with full per-DOF uncertainty (ours)\n  - \"reconvla\": ReconVLA baseline — scalar Euclidean argmin selection (plan_014)'''\nnew_baselines = '''Baselines:\n  - \"vla_only\": SmolVLA mean of K=10, no CRM refinement\n  - \"crm_scalar\": CRM with scalar uncertainty (single std, not per-DOF)\n  - \"crm_perdof\": CRM with full per-DOF uncertainty (ours)\n  - \"acr_vla\": alias for crm_perdof — the full ACR-VLA pipeline\n  - \"reconvla\": ReconVLA baseline — scalar Euclidean argmin selection (plan_014)'''\ncontent = content.replace(old_baselines, new_baselines)\n\n# 2. Add usage example\nold_usage = '  python evaluate.py --benchmark spatial --mode reconvla --n_rollouts 20'\nnew_usage = '''  python evaluate.py --benchmark spatial --mode reconvla --n_rollouts 20\n  python evaluate.py --benchmark spatial --mode acr_vla --crm_checkpoint /path/to/crm_best.pt --n_rollouts 10'''\ncontent = content.replace(old_usage, new_usage)\n\n# 3. Add acr_vla to mode choices\nold_choices = 'choices=[\"vla_only\", \"crm_scalar\", \"crm_perdof\", \"reconvla\"]'\nnew_choices = 'choices=[\"vla_only\", \"crm_scalar\", \"crm_perdof\", \"acr_vla\", \"reconvla\"]'\ncontent = content.replace(old_choices, new_choices)\n\n# 4. Add --crm_checkpoint argument (after --checkpoint)\nold_checkpoint_arg = '''    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str, default=None,\n                   help=\"Path to CRM checkpoint (crm_best.pt)\")'''\nnew_checkpoint_arg = '''    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str, default=None,\n                   help=\"Path to CRM checkpoint (crm_best.pt)\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--crm_checkpoint\", type=str, default=None,\n                   help=\"Alias for --checkpoint (used with --mode acr_vla)\")'''\ncontent = content.replace(old_checkpoint_arg, new_checkpoint_arg)\n\n# 5. Update CRM loading condition to include acr_vla\nold_crm_check = 'if args.mode in (\"crm_scalar\", \"crm_perdof\"):'\nnew_crm_check = 'if args.mode in (\"crm_scalar\", \"crm_perdof\", \"acr_vla\"):'\ncontent = content.replace(old_crm_check, new_crm_check)\n\n# 6. Add crm_checkpoint → checkpoint resolution and acr_vla → crm_perdof mapping\n# Insert right after set_seed and device lines in evaluate()\nold_eval_start = '''    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n\n    print(f\"[eval] Mode: {args.mode}, benchmark: {args.benchmark}\")'''\nnew_eval_start = '''    set_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n\n    # --crm_checkpoint is an alias for --checkpoint\n    if args.crm_checkpoint and not args.checkpoint:\n        args.checkpoint = args.crm_checkpoint\n\n    print(f\"[eval] Mode: {args.mode}, benchmark: {args.benchmark}\")'''\ncontent = content.replace(old_eval_start, new_eval_start)\n\n# 7. Update unc_dim else branch to also handle acr_vla explicitly\nold_unc_dim = '''        if args.mode == \"crm_scalar\":\n            unc_dim = 3 * H_EFF\n        else:\n            unc_dim = CORRECTION_DIM * H_EFF + H_EFF + H_EFF  # 40'''\nnew_unc_dim = '''        if args.mode == \"crm_scalar\":\n            unc_dim = 3 * H_EFF\n        else:  # crm_perdof or acr_vla\n            unc_dim = CORRECTION_DIM * H_EFF + H_EFF + H_EFF  # 40'''\ncontent = content.replace(old_unc_dim, new_unc_dim)\n\nwith open(\"/root/acr-vla-conformal-refinement/src/evaluate.py\", \"w\") as f:\n    f.write(content)\n\nprint(\"DONE - evaluate.py patched successfully\")\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/patch_evaluate.py",
  "account": "root"
}
